# Angular parity checklist

Generated by `bin/angular_parity_checklist` on 2026-10-16. Edit rows in place; regenerating needs `--force` and discards edits.

- Legacy baseline: `8ceb99e9a7` (pre-deangularization, Bootstrap 3 case workspace; live at http://localhost:3001, see [legacy_comparison.md](../legacy_comparison.md))
- Main: `776c68b535` (origin/main) · Branch: `8ce48fc3fa` (HEAD)

**Closing a row:** fill in the owner (ERB partial and Stimulus controller) and the covering scenario. Tick each extracted item that is at parity. For anything removed or changed on purpose, link the decision (NOT-DOING entry, feature doc heading, scenario or commit SHA). Tag losses that are defects `[MIGRATION]`, `[MIGRATION-FOLLOWUP]` or `[PREEXISTING]`, as defined in AGENTS.md. If no decision exists anywhere, record that as a finding; don't write one after the fact. A row is closed when every item is ticked or linked.

The legacy instance is the baseline. Items are extracted mechanically from the legacy source and compared with main's newest Angular copy (main's current copy, or the version just before main ported it). That copy is a second reference, not the source of truth: main's own Bootstrap 5 and Angular-removal commits can lose features too.

- _(added on main)_: main added it after legacy; the port must keep it.
- _(not in main)_: main's copy no longer has it. Still verify it against the legacy instance. If it's missing today, the row's main commits show whether a feature PR removed it deliberately or a migration commit lost it before this branch. A loss on main is triaged as an existing gap, not a branch blocker. Many are renames: `ng-class` or copy that changed shows as one _(not in main)_ item plus one _(added on main)_ item.

`{{…}}` interpolations and Angular-only plumbing will produce some noise: tick those as N/A.
Owner candidates are filename matches, not verified owners.

- **Used by (legacy):**`app/assets/javascripts/components/new_case/new_case_controller.js`, `app/assets/javascripts/controllers/wizardCtrl.js`
- **Owner candidates:** `app/views/shared/_wizard_modal.html.erb`
- **Owner:** `app/views/shared/_wizard_modal.html.erb`, `_wizard_engine_tile.html.erb`, `app/javascript/controllers/wizard_controller.js`
- **Scenario:** 3.2, 3.2b, 7 (Mapper Wizard handoff)
- **Decisions:** P1 restore `8ce48fc3` (tiles, ping it, troubleshooting, header types, field suggestions, Static guide). The typo confirm, Enter submission, required test query and Solr-only API method had no decision and are restored in this pass. The API method select is restored to Solr-only. Search API mapper evaluation during validation is restored (`applyMapperFunctions`): Current handed the endpoint's mapper code to the validator as a string, so a failing or missing mapper passed silently and no fields were discovered; replayed on Legacy and Current. The three remaining differences were decided on 2026-10-17: the static CSV preview and Import button (with success no longer shown in the error alert) and the collapsed Solr Configuration and Advanced sections are restored (`wizard_behaviour.spec.ts`); the Legacy frame is restored (no "Create a Quepid case" title bar or Cancel footer, added in `3f919211`; step dots along the bottom instead of the numbered tabs `b11ce954` put at the top); the create-new / use-existing accordion is restored (both panels closed on arrival, the TLS reload reopens the existing panel); the Search API query pattern is restored to the Query step (one-line input for GET, a textarea otherwise), seeded from the endpoint's test query, with "Query pattern is required." shown at once and Continue disabled while it is empty (`wizard_behaviour.spec.ts`). Checked by source against `8ceb99e9` and `776c68b5`, a live run on Current (3.2), and a Legacy (:3001) vs Current replay of the Endpoint, Search API, existing-endpoint and static CSV flows on 2026-10-17 (screenshots in `.playwright-mcp/wizard-parity/`).

- **Decisions:** Restored on 2026-10-17 (`tune_relevance.spec.ts`): the Evaluate Nightly? and Escape Queries tooltips, their Legacy order, the endpoint picker copy and empty state, the protocol-mismatch explanation, history shading and hover text, and the case toolbar on a try with an endpoint on the other protocol. Search Endpoints stays last, as Eric Pugh ordered it on main in #1775 (`334df0ea`). Kept: the Annotations intro copy. The Tuning Knobs empty-state note and tip are also restored ([NOT-DOING](../todo/NOT-DOING.md#angular-parity-changes-kept-after-the-2026-10-17-review)). Rerun keeping the drawer open matches Legacy. Ticked items were checked by source against `8ceb99e9` and `776c68b5`, and live on Current for the tabs, section defaults, knob edit and rerun, and Endpoint Details; the ES/OS, Vectara, Algolia and Search API editor modes, the nightly toggle, escape-queries visibility and the TLS reload were checked by source only. The "not currently used" knob message sits inside the `inQueryParams` block, so no version ever showed it.

- **Used by (legacy):**`app/assets/javascripts/components/judgements/judgements_controller.js`
- **Owner candidates:** `app/views/shared/_judgements_core_modal.html.erb`, `app/javascript/controllers/judgements_core_controller.js`
- **Owner:** `_judgements_core_modal.html.erb`, `_judgement_book_templates.html.erb`, `judgements_core_controller.js`
- **Scenario:** 6.6
- **Decisions:** Main redesigned this modal after Legacy (Case Integration card with auto-sync checkboxes and Populate Now / Refresh Ratings / Sync Queries; the Legacy "Populate Book" and "Refresh ratings from book" controls are `not in main`). Current's copy was compared word for word against main (`776c68b5`) and had drifted since the partial adaptation in `6cfe145f` (upstream `c468d18f`, `4a9065db`): intro paragraph, the no-books/no-teams alert, a leftover "You have not created any Books" line and the book-list help text. All restored to main's wording in this pass (`toolbar_modals_core.spec.ts` "judgements modal keeps the upstream copy" fails on the old copy). Legacy itself was replayed only for the toolbar link and the empty state because the Legacy database has no teams or books. Selecting, saving, retry, View navigation, disconnect and the sync toggles are covered live by `toolbar_modals_core.spec.ts` and `share_case.spec.ts`.
- **Re-verified 2026-10-23** after the large-case change (documents are built only for expanded queries; detailed export, book populate and the frog report read the live queries): Legacy case 4 and a Current case with the same 21 queries, 200 ratings, try and scorer, each with a "Parity Book" on a "Parity Team". Populate Book on Legacy and Populate Now on Current wrote the same 210 query/doc pairs with the same positions. The stored `document_fields` differed: Current also stored every raw response field (`id`, `catch_line`), because `buildQueryDocPairsPayload` merged `rawFields` (added in `4eb92933` as a workaround for the documents store's copies lacking `subsList`). Restored on 2026-10-23: Populate Now passes the live queries, as auto-sync does, and pairs store the title and displayed fields only, as on Legacy and main (`book_sync.test.js`).

- **Used by (legacy):**`app/assets/javascripts/components/export_case/export_case_controller.js`
- **Owner candidates:** `app/views/shared/_export_case_core_modal.html.erb`, `app/javascript/controllers/export_case_core_controller.js`
- **Owner:** `_export_case_core_modal.html.erb`, `export_case_core_controller.js`
- **Scenario:** 6.1
- **Decisions:** Compared with Legacy (:3001) and main (`776c68b5`): the modal text is identical word for word on Legacy and Current (live diff with snapshots present only on Current), the link targets resolve to the same JSON (`/api/cases/N?shallow=false`, `/queries`, `/annotations`, `/scores`, `/api/export/…`, `/api/docs`). Downloads replayed on both for Information Need, General, Detailed, Basic, TREC, RRE, LTR and Quepid JSON: same file names (`<case>_<format>.<ext>`) and same headers and first rows; the Snapshot format downloaded on Current (`<case>_snapshot.csv`, "Snapshot Name,Snapshot Time,Case ID,Query Text,Doc ID,Doc Position") but did not download on Legacy in this replay (not investigated: Legacy-only, unverified). The Quepid-format radio (`#quepid`, added on main) is present. **Evidence level:** ticked from the modal text diff and the download replay. The "This snapshot is currently being processed" item is ticked as N/A: Legacy's `isProcessingFile()` always returned false (its loop returned from the callback, so the warning never rendered), and Current has no equivalent. Not replayed: Basic or TREC with a snapshot selected.
- **Re-verified 2026-10-23** after the large-case change (documents are built only for expanded queries; detailed export, book populate and the frog report read the live queries): Detailed CSV downloaded on Legacy case 4 and a Current case with the same queries and ratings: 210 rows, same header, the same rows apart from the Team column (that case had no team) and one title with embedded double quotes, which Legacy wrote unwrapped (`Definition of ""supervise""…`, malformed CSV) and Current wraps correctly. Both order queries by query id.

- **Owner candidates:** `app/views/shared/_import_ratings_core_modal.html.erb`, `app/javascript/controllers/import_ratings_core_controller.js`
- **Owner:** `_import_ratings_core_modal.html.erb`, `import_ratings_core_controller.js`
- **Scenario:** 6.2
- **Decisions:** Replayed on Legacy and Current with disposable clones: modal text identical (live diff; tabs are buttons on Current, from main), the per-type "This operation WILL override…" warning, CSV preview, ratings CSV import and "Successfully imported ratings from CSV.", LTR import, information-needs import (and its missing-query error), snapshot import ("Snapshots imported successfully!"). Restored in this pass: the header-mismatch wording ("…in your file (check for correct spelling and capitalization)", "Required headers mismatch!" for snapshots) and the "Unable to import … from CSV/RRE/LTR" and "Could not import snapshots successfully!" prefixes (`case_import_ratings.spec.ts` fails on the old copy; Vitest cases added). The stricter CSV row validation is kept with Legacy's quoting hint added ([NOT-DOING](../todo/NOT-DOING.md#angular-parity-changes-kept-after-the-2026-10-19-review)); Legacy's "Header" switch was an Angular Material `md-switch` that never rendered, because that library isn't loaded (re-checked live 2026-10-19). A failed import keeps the modal open and shows the error inside it ([NOT-DOING](../todo/NOT-DOING.md#angular-parity-changes-kept-after-the-2026-10-19-review)). The RRE sample file returned a server 500 on both (see the RRE entry in `after-angular-removal.md`).

- **Used by (legacy):**`app/assets/javascripts/directives/searchResults.js`
- **Owner candidates:** `app/javascript/controllers/search_results_controller.js`
- **Owner:** `_query_list_templates.html.erb`, `search_results_controller.js`, `query_row_controller.js`
- **Scenario:** 4.15, 4.16, 4.20, 4.21, 4.22
- **Decisions:** Compared the Legacy template (`8ceb99e9`) with Current's `_query_list_templates.html.erb` item by item, and replayed on Legacy (:3001) and Current (:3000) with equivalent clones of "10s of Queries" on 2026-10-17: expand/collapse, Toggle Notes, Information Need and notes save (same success flash, persisted after reload, same "Info Need: …" hover tooltip), Peek at the next page (10 to 20 docs), Show only rated (10 rated docs, note and count), Score All set and RESET (every document follows), Copy query (clipboard holds the raw query text) and Delete Query. Intentional differences with a recorded decision: "Unrated" and "of 1.00" score states (user commit `7c304651`, scenario 4.7); Delete Query asks through the shared Confirm modal with the same copy (user commit `60d0beb4`, scenario 4.15); Browse opens the browse-query modal, not a new tab (main-only change, rows 21 and 39, scenario 4.18); the "Results" label under the count renders on main and Current but never rendered on Legacy (its self-closed `<count-up />` swallowed it). The error state is not reproducible on the live Solr (it tolerates malformed queries); it is covered by `core_failure_recovery.spec.ts` and `workspace_bootstrap.spec.ts`. **Evidence level:** every item was ticked from the template comparison; only the flows named above were replayed live. Not replayed: the Querqy icon, diff display with a query in error, the bootstrapping and updating banners, the disabled-Manual state, Show only rated disabled for unsupported engines, and the notes box `hidden`/`d-none` class itself.
- **Re-verified 2026-10-23** after the large-case change (documents are built only for expanded queries; detailed export, book populate and the frog report read the live queries): Same replay: all 21 query scores and result counts, the case score, the 10 results and their ratings, "Show only rated" (the same 10 rated docs), "Peek at the next page of results" (the same 20), rating a doc, and collapse then re-expand (results and the new rating kept) all match. Bar widths match; match labels differ only by the splainer-search 3.3.0 wording already recorded for row 19.

- **Owner:** `missing_documents_controller.js`, `shared/_dynamic_modal_templates.html.erb` (`missing-documents-modal-template`)
- **Scenario:** 4.9
- **Decisions:** Legacy (:3001) and Current (:3000) replayed on 2026-10-17: Search by Lucene `id:` returns the matching document under "Query Results" (Current) / "Your query … returned one matching document" (Legacy), a no-match search reports "Your query … returned no results", a document found can be rated, and Reset to All Rated Docs returns to the rated list. The Angular-era engine-specific help (Lucene syntax links, "click Search instead of Enter") was replaced on main by a pre-filled one-off query editor (JSON editor for ES/OS), and Solr document finding was restored with its own hint by user commit `795b2474` (scenario 4.9). That Solr finder was removed again in `9443d215` ("nicer find missing docs"), so Solr now opens on the try's params like the other engines; kept by user choice on 2026-10-24, see [NOT-DOING](../todo/NOT-DOING.md#missing-documents-on-solr-edits-the-trys-params) (`todo_fixes.spec.ts` covers it). "Unsupported engine" and the rated-lookup-unsupported notices are source-checked. Existing `core_event_actions.spec.ts` covers rating inside the modal.

- **Decisions:** Source comparison with `_queries.html.erb`, plus a live replay on Legacy and Current (2026-10-17): Name, Modified, Score (descending first, then reversed) and Errors sorts reorder the same way, the active sort shows its arrow, the sort is kept in the URL and survives reload on Current, the filter narrows the list, Collapse all closes every open query, and dragging a query in Manual order persists across reload on both. Current always writes `reverse=false` where Legacy wrote a bare `reverse` only when reversed; the two forms mean the same. The disabled-Manual explanation and the disabled Show only rated are rendered from the same flags (source only, not replayed). **Evidence level:** every item was ticked from the template comparison; only the flows named above were replayed live. Not replayed: the Manual-sorting-disabled popover, Show only rated disabled for unsupported engines, the bootstrapping and updating banners, and the `unsortable`/`dragging` list classes.

- **Owner:** `_diff_modal.html.erb`, `_snapshot_selection_template.html.erb`, `diff_core_controller.js`
- **Scenario:** 5.3
- **Decisions:** Replayed on Legacy (:3001) and Current (:3000) with snapshot clones on 2026-10-17: picker copy and controls, select, Update Comparison Settings, Clear Comparison View, inline "yes / no" delete confirmation, and the case and query diff badges. Two losses restored in this pass: the bold "Warning:" prefix on the duplicate-snapshot warning (now in `_diff_modal.html.erb`; `snapshots_and_annotations.spec.ts` asserts it and fails without the fix) and the "Loading available snapshots..." state while the list loads (`workspace_modal_lifecycle.spec.ts` "compare picker shows the snapshot loading state…" fails without the fix; Current showed the apply-time "Fetching snapshot data" text instead). The "-- Loading Snapshots --" placeholder option is not restored: no select exists until the list arrives, and the alert says the same thing. Intro copy says "1-5" where Legacy said "1-3"; Legacy allowed five (`queryViewSvc.getMaxSnapshots()`), so the copy is a correction. Buttons are `type=button` with Stimulus actions in place of the Angular submit handlers.

- **Scenario:** 6.9
- **Decisions:** Replayed on Legacy case 4 and Current case 6: the summary, congratulation, distribution chart and Close match. One loss restored in this pass: Legacy worded the zero-result count as "none return", "only one returns" or "N return" before "zero results"; Current always said "0 return". Restored (`frog_report_controller.js`, `_dynamic_modal_templates.html.erb`; Vitest "words the zero-result count…" fails without it, and `workspace_modal_lifecycle.spec.ts` "Frog Report words the zero-result count as Legacy did" fails on the old copy). Refresh from book and its error state are covered live by `workspace_modal_lifecycle.spec.ts`.
- **Re-verified 2026-10-23** after the large-case change (documents are built only for expanded queries; detailed export, book populate and the frog report read the live queries): Same replay with one unrated query added to both: summary text identical (21 queries, 210 ratings needed, 10 missing, 4.8% missing rate) and the same chart buckets. One difference not caught before: Legacy shows a bar's value only on hover (its text mark reads `tooltip.amount`), Current labels every bar. Kept by user choice for accessibility; see [NOT-DOING](../todo/NOT-DOING.md#frog-report-labels-every-bar).

The page rewords the same explanation ("Case N isn't available… deleted, or not shared with a team you belong to") and adds View all cases / View your teams / Create a case buttons, since Legacy's toast left a dead end (decided 2026-10-19; scenario 4.17, `core_controller_test.rb`, `case_shell_navigation.spec.ts`).

### 103. `app/assets/javascripts/components/share_scorer/share_scorer.html`

- **Kind:** template
- **Ported:** main `e52cec3a` — Migrate teams, scorers and case listings away from angularjs (#1642)
- **Before the port:** unchanged since legacy (compared with `e52cec3a^`)
- **Used by (legacy):**`app/assets/javascripts/components/share_scorer/share_scorer_directive.js`
- **Owner candidates:** `app/views/shared/_share_scorer_modal.html.erb`, `app/javascript/controllers/share_scorer_controller.js`
- **Owner:** `shared/_share_scorer_modal.html.erb`
- **Scenario:** 8.7
- **Decisions:** Dead at Legacy: this AngularJS component was only used by `teams/show.html` or another Angular management view, but Legacy (`8ceb99e9`) serves `/teams`, `/teams/:id`, `/scorers`, `/search_endpoints`, `/books` and their forms as Rails pages with no AngularJS on them (live check: no `ng-app`, no `angular` global). Those Rails pages are what main and Current serve: Legacy, main (:3003) and Current (:3000) were compared by text on 2026-10-17 and differ only by main's later changes (AI Judges nav item, New Team/Scorer buttons, share-help wording, data). Main and Current were also compared modal by modal (Share, Archive, Remove member Confirm modals are identical).

- **Kind:** behaviour-only directive (no markup)

- **Owner candidates:** _none found_
- **Owner:** `_tune_relevance.html.erb` (knob inputs)
- **Scenario:** 4.11
- **Decisions:** Legacy used `auto-grow` only on the Tuning Knobs value input (`devQueryParams.html`). Current has no auto-grow (source); removed on main in `a3e87c89` and kept ([NOT-DOING](../todo/NOT-DOING.md#angular-parity-changes-kept-after-the-2026-10-17-review)). The input no longer clips long values: it sizes to its value between Legacy's auto-grow limits through CSS `field-sizing`, with a 14ch fallback (`tune_relevance.spec.ts`, 2026-10-19).
